                ), as_fieldop(λ(__arg0_____, __arg1) → ·__arg0_____ × ·__arg1,
                              u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩)(
                     as_fieldop(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 2ₒ⟫(__it),
                                u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩)(
                       __ct_el_5
                     ), as_fieldop(λ(it) → list_get(2, ·it), u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[ ⟩)(
                          primal_normal_vert_y
                        )
                   )
              ),
              as_fieldop(λ(__arg0_____, __arg1) → ·__arg0_____ + ·__arg1,
                         u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩)(
                as_fieldop(λ(__arg0_____, __arg1) → ·__arg0_____ × ·__arg1,
                           u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩)(
                  as_fieldop(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 3ₒ⟫(__it),
                             u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩)(
                    __ct_el_4
                  ), as_fieldop(λ(it) → list_get(3, ·it), u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[ ⟩)(
                       primal_normal_vert_x
                     )
                ), as_fieldop(λ(__arg0_____, __arg1) → ·__arg0_____ × ·__arg1,
                              u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩)(
                     as_fieldop(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 3ₒ⟫(__it),
                                u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩)(
                       __ct_el_5
                     ), as_fieldop(λ(it) → list_get(3, ·it), u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[ ⟩)(
                          primal_normal_vert_y
                        )
                   )
              )
            ))(as_fieldop(
                 λ(__arg0) → cast_(·__arg0, float64), u⟨ Vertexₕ: [0, 5159[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩
               )(u_vert),
               as_fieldop(
                 λ(__arg0) → cast_(·__arg0, float64), u⟨ Vertexₕ: [0, 5159[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩
               )(v_vert));
}

============================================================
=== GTIR AFTER INLINING LIFTS AND LAMBDAS ===

calculate_nabla2_and_smag_coefficients_for_vn(

  kh_smag_ec,

  smag_offset,
  horizontal_start,

  vertical_start,
  vertical_end
) {
  {kh_smag_e, kh_smag_ec, z_nabla2_e} @ {u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩,
                                         u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩,
                                         u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩}
       ← as_fieldop(
           λ(
             diff_multfac_smag,
             u_vert,
             primal_normal_vert_x,
             v_vert,
             primal_normal_vert_y,
             inv_vert_vert_length,
             dual_normal_vert_x,
             dual_normal_vert_y,
             tangent_orientation,
             inv_primal_edge_length,
             __iasfop_38,
             smag_limit,
             vn
           ) → 
             (λ(_cs_117, _cs_118, _cs_119, _cs_120, _cs_121, _cs_122, _cs_123, _cs_124, _cs_125) → 
                (λ(_cs_102, _cs_103, _cs_104, _cs_105, _cs_107, _cs_108) → 
                   (λ(_cs_100, _cs_101) → 
                      (λ(_cs_92, _cs_93) → {minimum(maximum(cast_(_cs_92 - ·__iasfop_38, float64), 0.0), ·smag_limit),
                                            cast_(_cs_92, float64),
                                            ((_cs_108 + _cs_107 - _cs_93) × (_cs_117 × _cs_117)
                                             + (_cs_105 + _cs_104 - _cs_93) × (_cs_102 × _cs_102)) × 4.0})(
                        cast_(·diff_multfac_smag, float64) × sqrt(_cs_100 × _cs_100 + _cs_101 × _cs_101), ·vn × 2.0
                      ))((_cs_104 - _cs_105) × _cs_102
                         - (_cs_122 × list_get(1, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_123 × list_get(1, ·dual_normal_vert_y)
                            - (_cs_124 × list_get(0, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_125 × list_get(0, ·dual_normal_vert_y)))
                           × _cs_103,
                         (_cs_107 - _cs_108) × _cs_103
                         + (_cs_118 × list_get(3, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_119 × list_get(3, ·dual_normal_vert_y)
                            - (_cs_120 × list_get(2, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_121 × list_get(2, ·dual_normal_vert_y)))
                           × _cs_102))(
                  ·inv_vert_vert_length,
                  ·tangent_orientation × _cs_117,
                  _cs_118 × list_get(3, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_119 × list_get(3, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_120 × list_get(2, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_121 × list_get(2, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_122 × list_get(1, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_123 × list_get(1, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_124 × list_get(0, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_125 × list_get(0, ·primal_normal_vert_y)
                ))(·inv_primal_edge_length,
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 3ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 3ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 2ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 2ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 0ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 0ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64)),
           u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩
         )(diff_multfac_smag,
           u_vert,
           primal_normal_vert_x,

============================================================
=== FINAL GTIR HANDED TO GTFN BACKEND ===
============================================================
calculate_nabla2_and_smag_coefficients_for_vn(
  diff_multfac_smag,
  tangent_orientation,
  inv_primal_edge_length,
  inv_vert_vert_length,
  u_vert,
  v_vert,
  primal_normal_vert_x,
  primal_normal_vert_y,
  dual_normal_vert_x,
  dual_normal_vert_y,
  vn,
  smag_limit,
  kh_smag_e,
  kh_smag_ec,
  z_nabla2_e,
  smag_offset,
  horizontal_start,
  horizontal_end,
  vertical_start,
  vertical_end
) {
  _calculate_nabla2_and_smag_coefficients_for_vn = λ(
    diff_multfac_smag,
    tangent_orientation,
    inv_primal_edge_length,
    inv_vert_vert_length,
    u_vert,
    v_vert,
    primal_normal_vert_x,
    primal_normal_vert_y,
    dual_normal_vert_x,
    dual_normal_vert_y,
    vn,
    smag_limit,
    smag_offset
  ) → 
    (λ(__tuple_tmp_0) → 
       (λ(diff_multfac_smag_wpᐞ0) → 
          (λ(u_vert_wpᐞ0) → 
             (λ(v_vert_wpᐞ0) → 
                (λ(smag_offset_wpᐞ0) → 
                   (λ(v_n_0ᐞ0) → 
                      (λ(v_n_1ᐞ0) → 
                         (λ(v_n_2ᐞ0) → 
                            (λ(v_n_3ᐞ0) → 
                               (λ(nabla2_of_vnᐞ0) → 
                                  (λ(nabla2_of_vnᐞ1) → 
                                     (λ(v_t_0ᐞ0) → 
                                        (λ(v_t_1ᐞ0) → 
                                           (λ(v_t_2ᐞ0) → 
                                              (λ(v_t_3ᐞ0) → 
                                                 (λ(l_pᐞ0) → 
                                                    (λ(l_vvᐞ0) → 
                                                       (λ(kh_smag_part1ᐞ0) → 
                                                          (λ(kh_smag_part2ᐞ0) → 
                                                             (λ(kh_smag_wpᐞ0) → 
                                                                (λ(kh_smag_vp_for_diffusionᐞ0) →

============================================================
=== GTIR AFTER INLINING LIFTS AND LAMBDAS ===
============================================================
calculate_nabla2_and_smag_coefficients_for_vn(
  diff_multfac_smag,
  tangent_orientation,
  inv_primal_edge_length,
  inv_vert_vert_length,
  u_vert,
  v_vert,
  primal_normal_vert_x,
  primal_normal_vert_y,
  dual_normal_vert_x,
  dual_normal_vert_y,
  vn,
  smag_limit,
  kh_smag_e,
  kh_smag_ec,
  z_nabla2_e,
  smag_offset,
  horizontal_start,
  horizontal_end,
  vertical_start,
  vertical_end
) {
  {kh_smag_e, kh_smag_ec, z_nabla2_e} @ {u⟨ Edgeₕ: [1664, 15190[, Kᵥ: [0, 5[ ⟩,
                                         u⟨ Edgeₕ: [1664, 15190[, Kᵥ: [0, 5[ ⟩,
                                         u⟨ Edgeₕ: [1664, 15190[, Kᵥ: [0, 5[ ⟩}
       ← as_fieldop(
           λ(
             diff_multfac_smag,
             u_vert,
             primal_normal_vert_x,
             v_vert,
             primal_normal_vert_y,
             inv_vert_vert_length,
             dual_normal_vert_x,
             dual_normal_vert_y,
             tangent_orientation,
             inv_primal_edge_length,
             __iasfop_38,
             smag_limit,
             vn
           ) → 
             (λ(_cs_117, _cs_118, _cs_119, _cs_120, _cs_121, _cs_122, _cs_123, _cs_124, _cs_125) → 
                (λ(_cs_102, _cs_103, _cs_104, _cs_105, _cs_107, _cs_108) → 
                   (λ(_cs_100, _cs_101) → 
                      (λ(_cs_92, _cs_93) → {minimum(maximum(cast_(_cs_92 - ·__iasfop_38, float64), 0.0), ·smag_limit),
                                            cast_(_cs_92, float64),
                                            ((_cs_108 + _cs_107 - _cs_93) × (_cs_117 × _cs_117)
                                             + (_cs_105 + _cs_104 - _cs_93) × (_cs_102 × _cs_102)) × 4.0})(
                        cast_(·diff_multfac_smag, float64) × sqrt(_cs_100 × _cs_100 + _cs_101 × _cs_101), ·vn × 2.0
                      ))((_cs_104 - _cs_105) × _cs_102
                         - (_cs_122 × list_get(1, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_123 × list_get(1, ·dual_normal_vert_y)
                            - (_cs_124 × list_get(0, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_125 × list_get(0, ·dual_normal_vert_y)))
                           × _cs_103,
                         (_cs_107 - _cs_108) × _cs_103
                         + (_cs_118 × list_get(3, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_119 × list_get(3, ·dual_normal_vert_y)
                            - (_cs_120 × list_get(2, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_121 × list_get(2, ·dual_normal_vert_y)))
                           × _cs_102))(
                  ·inv_vert_vert_length,
                  ·tangent_orientation × _cs_117,
                  _cs_118 × list_get(3, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_119 × list_get(3, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_120 × list_get(2, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_121 × list_get(2, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_122 × list_get(1, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_123 × list_get(1, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_124 × list_get(0, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_125 × list_get(0, ·primal_normal_vert_y)
                ))(·inv_primal_edge_length,
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 3ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 3ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 2ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 2ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 0ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 0ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64)), u⟨ Edgeₕ: [1664, 15190[, Kᵥ: [0, 5[ ⟩
         )(diff_multfac_smag,
           u_vert,
           primal_normal_vert_x,
           v_vert,
           primal_normal_vert_y,
           inv_vert_vert_length,
           dual_normal_vert_x,
           dual_normal_vert_y,
           tangent_orientation,
           inv_primal_edge_length,
           cast_(smag_offset, float64),
           smag_limit,
           vn);
}

============================================================
=== GTIR AFTER COMMON TRANSFORMS ===
============================================================
calculate_nabla2_and_smag_coefficients_for_vn(
  diff_multfac_smag,
  tangent_orientation,
  inv_primal_edge_length,
  inv_vert_vert_length,

============================================================
=== GTIR AFTER INLINING LIFTS AND LAMBDAS ===
============================================================
calculate_nabla2_and_smag_coefficients_for_vn(
  diff_multfac_smag,
  tangent_orientation,
  inv_primal_edge_length,
  inv_vert_vert_length,
  u_vert,
  v_vert,
  primal_normal_vert_x,
  primal_normal_vert_y,
  dual_normal_vert_x,
  dual_normal_vert_y,
  vn,
  smag_limit,
  kh_smag_e,
  kh_smag_ec,
  z_nabla2_e,
  smag_offset,
  horizontal_start,
  horizontal_end,
  vertical_start,
  vertical_end
) {
  {kh_smag_e, kh_smag_ec, z_nabla2_e} @ {u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [0, 5[ ⟩,
                                         u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [0, 5[ ⟩,
                                         u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [0, 5[ ⟩}
       ← as_fieldop(
           λ(
             diff_multfac_smag,
             u_vert,
             primal_normal_vert_x,
             v_vert,
             primal_normal_vert_y,
             inv_vert_vert_length,
             dual_normal_vert_x,
             dual_normal_vert_y,
             tangent_orientation,
             inv_primal_edge_length,
             __iasfop_38,
             smag_limit,
             vn
           ) → 
             (λ(_cs_117, _cs_118, _cs_119, _cs_120, _cs_121, _cs_122, _cs_123, _cs_124, _cs_125) → 
                (λ(_cs_102, _cs_103, _cs_104, _cs_105, _cs_107, _cs_108) → 
                   (λ(_cs_100, _cs_101) → 
                      (λ(_cs_92, _cs_93) → {minimum(maximum(cast_(_cs_92 - ·__iasfop_38, float64), 0.0), ·smag_limit),
                                            cast_(_cs_92, float64),
                                            ((_cs_108 + _cs_107 - _cs_93) × (_cs_117 × _cs_117)
                                             + (_cs_105 + _cs_104 - _cs_93) × (_cs_102 × _cs_102)) × 4.0})(
                        cast_(·diff_multfac_smag, float64) × sqrt(_cs_100 × _cs_100 + _cs_101 × _cs_101), ·vn × 2.0
                      ))((_cs_104 - _cs_105) × _cs_102
                         - (_cs_122 × list_get(1, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_123 × list_get(1, ·dual_normal_vert_y)
                            - (_cs_124 × list_get(0, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_125 × list_get(0, ·dual_normal_vert_y)))
                           × _cs_103,
                         (_cs_107 - _cs_108) × _cs_103
                         + (_cs_118 × list_get(3, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_119 × list_get(3, ·dual_normal_vert_y)
                            - (_cs_120 × list_get(2, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_121 × list_get(2, ·dual_normal_vert_y)))
                           × _cs_102))(
                  ·inv_vert_vert_length,
                  ·tangent_orientation × _cs_117,
                  _cs_118 × list_get(3, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_119 × list_get(3, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_120 × list_get(2, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_121 × list_get(2, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_122 × list_get(1, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_123 × list_get(1, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_124 × list_get(0, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_125 × list_get(0, ·primal_normal_vert_y)
                ))(·inv_primal_edge_length,
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 3ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 3ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 2ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 2ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 0ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 0ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64)), u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [0, 5[ ⟩
         )(diff_multfac_smag,
           u_vert,
           primal_normal_vert_x,
           v_vert,
           primal_normal_vert_y,
           inv_vert_vert_length,
           dual_normal_vert_x,
           dual_normal_vert_y,
           tangent_orientation,
           inv_primal_edge_length,
           cast_(smag_offset, float64),
           smag_limit,
           vn);
}

============================================================
=== GTIR AFTER COMMON TRANSFORMS ===
============================================================
calculate_nabla2_and_smag_coefficients_for_vn(
  diff_multfac_smag,
  tangent_orientation,
  inv_primal_edge_length,
  inv_vert_vert_length,
  u_vert,
  v_vert,
  primal_normal_vert_x,
  primal_normal_vert_y,
  dual_normal_vert_x,
  dual_normal_vert_y,
  vn,
  smag_limit,
  kh_smag_e,
  kh_smag_ec,
  z_nabla2_e,
  smag_offset,
  horizontal_start,
  horizontal_end,
  vertical_start,
  vertical_end
) {
  {kh_smag_e, kh_smag_ec, z_nabla2_e} @ u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [0, 5[ ⟩
       ← as_fieldop(
           λ(
             diff_multfac_smag,
             u_vert,
             primal_normal_vert_x,
             v_vert,
             primal_normal_vert_y,
             inv_vert_vert_length,
             dual_normal_vert_x,
             dual_normal_vert_y,
             tangent_orientation,
             inv_primal_edge_length,
             __iasfop_38,
             smag_limit,
             vn
           ) → 
             (λ(_cs_117, _cs_118, _cs_119, _cs_120, _cs_121, _cs_122, _cs_123, _cs_124, _cs_125) → 
                (λ(_cs_102, _cs_103, _cs_104, _cs_105, _cs_107, _cs_108) → 
                   (λ(_cs_100, _cs_101) → 
                      (λ(_cs_92, _cs_93) → {minimum(maximum(cast_(_cs_92 - ·__iasfop_38, float64), 0.0), ·smag_limit),
                                            cast_(_cs_92, float64),
                                            ((_cs_108 + _cs_107 - _cs_93) × (_cs_117 × _cs_117)
                                             + (_cs_105 + _cs_104 - _cs_93) × (_cs_102 × _cs_102)) × 4.0})(
                        cast_(·diff_multfac_smag, float64) × sqrt(_cs_100 × _cs_100 + _cs_101 × _cs_101), ·vn × 2.0
                      ))((_cs_104 - _cs_105) × _cs_102
                         - (_cs_122 × list_get(1, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_123 × list_get(1, ·dual_normal_vert_y)
                            - (_cs_124 × list_get(0, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_125 × list_get(0, ·dual_normal_vert_y)))
                           × _cs_103,
                         (_cs_107 - _cs_108) × _cs_103
                         + (_cs_118 × list_get(3, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_119 × list_get(3, ·dual_normal_vert_y)
                            - (_cs_120 × list_get(2, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_121 × list_get(2, ·dual_normal_vert_y)))
                           × _cs_102))(
                  ·inv_vert_vert_length,
                  ·tangent_orientation × _cs_117,
                  _cs_118 × list_get(3, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_119 × list_get(3, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_120 × list_get(2, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_121 × list_get(2, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_122 × list_get(1, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_123 × list_get(1, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_124 × list_get(0, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_125 × list_get(0, ·primal_normal_vert_y)
                ))(·inv_primal_edge_length,
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 3ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 3ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 2ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 2ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 0ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 0ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64)), u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [0, 5[ ⟩
         )(diff_multfac_smag,
           u_vert,
           primal_normal_vert_x,
           v_vert,
           primal_normal_vert_y,
           inv_vert_vert_length,
           dual_normal_vert_x,
           dual_normal_vert_y,
           tangent_orientation,
           inv_primal_edge_length,
           cast_(smag_offset, float64),
           smag_limit,
           vn);

  smag_offset,
  horizontal_start,
  horizontal_end,
  vertical_start,
  vertical_end
) {
  {kh_smag_e, kh_smag_ec, z_nabla2_e} @ u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [0, 5[ ⟩
       ← as_fieldop(
           λ(
             diff_multfac_smag,
             u_vert,
             primal_normal_vert_x,
             v_vert,
             primal_normal_vert_y,
             inv_vert_vert_length,
             dual_normal_vert_x,
             dual_normal_vert_y,
             tangent_orientation,
             inv_primal_edge_length,
             __iasfop_38,
             smag_limit,
             vn
           ) → 
             (λ(_cs_117, _cs_118, _cs_119, _cs_120, _cs_121, _cs_122, _cs_123, _cs_124, _cs_125) → 
                (λ(_cs_102, _cs_103, _cs_104, _cs_105, _cs_107, _cs_108) → 
                   (λ(_cs_100, _cs_101) → 
                      (λ(_cs_92, _cs_93) → {minimum(maximum(cast_(_cs_92 - ·__iasfop_38, float64), 0.0), ·smag_limit),
                                            cast_(_cs_92, float64),
                                            ((_cs_108 + _cs_107 - _cs_93) × (_cs_117 × _cs_117)
                                             + (_cs_105 + _cs_104 - _cs_93) × (_cs_102 × _cs_102)) × 4.0})(
                        cast_(·diff_multfac_smag, float64) × sqrt(_cs_100 × _cs_100 + _cs_101 × _cs_101), ·vn × 2.0
                      ))((_cs_104 - _cs_105) × _cs_102
                         - (_cs_122 × list_get(1, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_123 × list_get(1, ·dual_normal_vert_y)
                            - (_cs_124 × list_get(0, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_125 × list_get(0, ·dual_normal_vert_y)))
                           × _cs_103,
                         (_cs_107 - _cs_108) × _cs_103
                         + (_cs_118 × list_get(3, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_119 × list_get(3, ·dual_normal_vert_y)
                            - (_cs_120 × list_get(2, ·dual_normal_vert_x) + _cs_121 × list_get(2, ·dual_normal_vert_y)))
                           × _cs_102))(
                  ·inv_vert_vert_length,
                  ·tangent_orientation × _cs_117,
                  _cs_118 × list_get(3, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_119 × list_get(3, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_120 × list_get(2, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_121 × list_get(2, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_122 × list_get(1, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_123 × list_get(1, ·primal_normal_vert_y),
                  _cs_124 × list_get(0, ·primal_normal_vert_x) + _cs_125 × list_get(0, ·primal_normal_vert_y)
                ))(·inv_primal_edge_length,
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 3ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 3ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 2ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 2ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 0ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64),
                   cast_(·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 0ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64)), u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [0, 5[ ⟩
         )(diff_multfac_smag,
           u_vert,
           primal_normal_vert_x,
           v_vert,
           primal_normal_vert_y,
           inv_vert_vert_length,
           dual_normal_vert_x,
           dual_normal_vert_y,
           tangent_orientation,
           inv_primal_edge_length,
           cast_(smag_offset, float64),
           smag_limit,
           vn);
}
